data-toggle="modal" data-bibtex="%40misc%7B%0Aupadhyay2026fedsgm%2C%0Atitle%3D%7BFed%7BSGM%7D%3A%20A%20Unified%20Framework%20for%20Constraint%20Aware%2C%20Bidirectionally%20Compressed%2C%20Multi-Step%20Federated%20Optimization%7D%2C%0Aauthor%3D%7BAntesh%20Upadhyay%20and%20Sang%20Bin%20Moon%20and%20Abolfazl%20Hashemi%7D%2C%0Ayear%3D%7B2026%7D%2C%0Aurl%3D%7Bhttps%3A%2F%2Fopenreview.net%2Fforum%3Fid%3DY6lVVJHiwN%7D%0A%7D"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">BibTeX</font></font></a></span><span class="item"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-copyright-mark " data-original-title="Licensed under Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">CC BY 4.0</font></font></a></span></div><div class="invitation-buttons"></div></div><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">关键词</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">联邦学习、分布式优化、约束优化</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">抽象的</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们提出了 FedSGM，一个统一的联邦约束优化框架，旨在解决联邦学习 (FL) 中的四大挑战：函数约束、通信瓶颈、局部更新和部分客户端参与。FedSGM 基于切换梯度法，提供无投影、仅原始变量的更新，避免了昂贵的对偶变量调优或内部求解器。为了应对通信限制，FedSGM 引入了双向误差反馈，在明确理解压缩噪声和多步局部更新之间相互作用的同时，校正了压缩引入的偏差。我们推导出了收敛性保证，表明平均迭代结果达到规范最优解。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="175" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-texatom texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi class="mjx-cal mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c4F TEX-C"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c221A"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-surd><mjx-box style="padding-top: 0.169em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D447 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-box></mjx-sqrt></mjx-msqrt></mjx-den></mjx-row></mjx-dtable></mjx-dbox></mjx-frac></mjx-mfrac><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><mi data-mjx-variant="-tex-calligraphic" mathvariant="script"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">哦</font></font></mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（</font></font></mo><mfrac><mn><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">1</font></font></mn><msqrt><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">T</font></font></mi></msqrt></mfrac><mo stretchy="false"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">）</font></font></mo></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">FedSGM 具有更高的速率，并引入了额外的高概率边界，将优化过程与部分参与导致的采样噪声解耦。此外，我们引入了 FedSGM 的软切换版本，以稳定可行性边界附近的更新。据我们所知，FedSGM 是第一个统一函数约束、压缩、多次局部更新和部分客户端参与的框架，为约束联邦学习建立了理论基础。最后，我们通过在 Neyman-Pearson 分类和约束马尔可夫决策过程 (CMDP) 任务上的实验验证了 FedSGM 的理论保证。</font></font></p>

inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">一些主要问题包括</font></font></p>
<ol>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">有限的实验</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">用于提高沟通效率的技术并非新事物。</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">实际应用价值有限</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">凸性假设具有局限性</font></font></li>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">几种现有技术的融合缺乏技术创新性。</font></font></li>
</ol>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">审稿人关注的问题</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">创新性有限（结合了几种现有技术）以及凸性假设的限制性问题尚未得到充分解决。</font></font></p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论者评分</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">未更改</font></font></p>

inherit;">≡</font></font></button></div><div class="heading"><h4><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">总体评价 - SOTA 与其他技术的比较（第 1/3 部分）</font></font></strong></h4><button type="button" class="btn btn-xs permalink-btn" aria-label="复制备注 0STLyCApw8 的 URL"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-link " data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" aria-hidden="true" data-original-title="Copy URL of note 0STLyCApw8"></span><span class="sr-only"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" URL</font></font></span></button></div><div class="subheading"><span class="invitation highlight" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" style="background-color: rgb(187, 187, 255); color: rgb(44, 58, 74);" data-original-title="Reply type"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">官方评论</font></font></span><span class="signatures"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">作者</font><span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">&ZeroWidthSpace;</font></span></font> <span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font></span></span><span class="created-date" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Date created"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-calendar " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">2025年11月22日 00:36（修改于2026年2月13日 01:36）</font></font></span><span class="readers" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="" data-original-title="Visible to &lt;br/&gt;everyone"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-eye-open " aria-hidden="true"></span><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">每个人</font></font></span><span class="revisions"><span class="glyphicon glyphicon-duplicate " aria-hidden="true"></span><a href="/revisions?id=0STLyCApw8"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">修订</font></font></a></span></div><div class="note-content-container "><div class="note-content"><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">评论</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们衷心感谢所有审稿人和领域主席抽出宝贵时间并提出建设性意见。审稿人认为我们的工作“严谨、全面且动机充分”（RqPZ），“意义重大且切题，值得单独发表”（BCyy），以及“严谨、文笔流畅且结构清晰”（Q7y9），这令我们倍感鼓舞。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">一些问题围绕着约束公式的新颖性、与投影或基于惩罚的方法的关系，以及缺乏与某些基线方法的比较展开。我们将在下文中一并讨论所有这些问题，因为它们都源于一个根本问题：</font></font><strong><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="176" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D446 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D43A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D440 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">F</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">e</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">S</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">G</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">M</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">这是首个能够同时且可证明地解决功能约束、多步局部更新、带误差反馈的双向压缩以及部分参与联邦学习等问题的方法。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">现有方法不适用于此场景，因此与它们进行直接比较既不可行也不相关。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为了从根本上审视问题并认识到我们方法的创新性，我们首先阐明</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">集合约束</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">和</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">函数约束</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">之间的区别。标准的集合约束优化通常假设约束集“简单”。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="177" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-texatom texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi class="mjx-cal mjx-b"><mjx-c class="mjx-c58 TEX-CB"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><mi data-mjx-variant="-tex-bold-calligraphic" mathvariant="bold-script"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">X</font></font></mi></mrow></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（例如，一个盒子或球体）其中投影计算成本很低。然而，在我们的工作中，我们关注的是如下形式的函数约束。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="178" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c28"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D464 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c29"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mo class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2264"></mjx-c></mjx-mo><mjx-mn class="mjx-n" space="4"><mjx-c class="mjx-c30"></mjx-c></mjx-mn></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi><mo stretchy="false"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（</font></font></mo><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">西</font></font></mi><mo stretchy="false"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">）</font></font></mo><mo><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">≤</font></font></mo><mn><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">0</font></font></mn></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">就像我们 NP 分类实验中的损失函数一样。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">我们将详细探讨审稿人提出的现有方法，特别是</font></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">投影法、惩罚法和弗兰克-沃尔夫法。</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">请参阅各章节以获取详细分析。</font></font></p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为什么</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="179" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D446 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D43A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D440 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">F</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">e</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">S</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">G</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">M</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">与投影法或近端法不具可比性？</font></font></strong></p>
</blockquote>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">对于这种公式，标准算法是近端梯度下降法（PGD），如 MURANA 论文 [1] 中所述。PGD 步骤如下：</font></font></p>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">本文旨在开发一种避免这种棘手投影的方法。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="193" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-texatom texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D446 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D43A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D440 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">F</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">e</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">S</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">G</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">M</font></font></mi></mrow></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">框架明确地不包含投影，并且只包含原始数据。</font></font></p>

<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为什么基于惩罚的 FedAvg 不是合适的基准？</font></font></strong></p>
</blockquote>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">请考虑以下基于惩罚的公式来代替本文中的问题（1），

xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">ρ</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">非常大。然而，制作</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="204" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70C TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">ρ</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">过大会导致不良条件，减缓优化速度。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">另一方面，任何固定的</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="205" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70C TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">ρ</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">太小的赔偿额可能导致持续的侵权行为。“正确”</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="206" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70C TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">ρ</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">取决于相对尺度和几何形状</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="207" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2207"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D453 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">∇</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">f</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">和</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="208" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c2207"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">∇</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">。</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="209" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-texatom texclass="ORD"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D446 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D43A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D440 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-texatom></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">F</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">e</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">S</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">G</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">M</font></font></mi></mrow></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"></font><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">它通过改变方向</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（目标函数与约束条件）来避免超参数调优</font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">，也就是说，无需调整惩罚权重，也不会出现条件数暴涨的情况。因此，这种基于惩罚的 FedAvg 算法并非合适的基准方法。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">然而，为了阐明上述要点，我们创建启发式方法来运行基于惩罚的联邦算法，其中在每一轮中，客户端都会更新它们的梯度。</font></font></p>

<blockquote>
<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为什么不适合与弗兰克-沃尔夫进行比较？</font></font></strong></p>
</blockquote>

inherit;">）</font></font></mo></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">约束边界由分布式客户端定义。因此，求解 LMO 问题需要一个迭代子程序（例如投影梯度下降法），其中每次内部迭代都需要一次完整的通信。因此，与基于投影的方法一样，Frank-Wolfe 方法在函数约束机制下也存在通信限制。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">由于联邦学习中现有的方法无法同时支持部分参与、局部更新和带误差反馈的压缩等功能约束，因此无法进行直接比较。我们的工作弥补了这一空白，通过刻画联邦学习中具有充分意义的各种因素之间的相互作用，建立了一个切实可行的、功能约束的框架。</font></font></p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">利用随机梯度下降法、深度神经网络和异构数据分布进行公平分类</font></font></strong></p>
</blockquote>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">为了进一步响应使用强基线和异构数据分布进行更多实验的呼吁，我们引入了一个新的公平分类实验，该实验在 ADULT 数据集上使用深度神经网络，并考虑了异构（非独立同分布）数据分布和随机梯度。请参见图</font></font><a href="https://imgur.com/ppeEMIt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">2</font></font></a><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">通过与 Penalty-FedAvg 和 IPP-ConEx（集中式 SOTA，[1]）进行明确比较，我们证明了</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="221" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D439 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D452 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D451 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D446 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D43A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D440 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">F</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">e</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">d</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">S</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">G</font></font></mi><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">M</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">该方法在目标函数满足性和约束条件满足性之间实现了最佳平衡，避免了因惩罚项调整而导致的不稳定性。实验的完整细节见附录（F）。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">参考：</font></font></p>
<ol>
<li><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">Boob 等人，“凸函数和非凸函数约束优化的随机一阶方法”（数学规划 2022）。</font></font></li>
</ol>

<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">更完善的公式和更深入的理论分析</font></font></strong></p>

<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">此外，我们同意审稿人的观点，即把分析扩展到随机数据（SGD）和非凸设置，可以显著提高我们框架在现代深度学习场景中的适用性。受审稿人建议的启发，我们在附录（D 和 E）中新增了两个定理。</font></font></p>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">首先，我们分析了</font></font><strong><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="227" style="font-size: 121.3%; style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">（</font></font></mo><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><mover><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font </ol>
</blockquote>
<p><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">此外，我们还添加了收敛结果。</font></font><strong><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="245" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D70C TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">ρ</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">本文研究了具有凸约束的弱凸目标函数</font></font></strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">在部分参与和局部更新下的问题。这涵盖了神经网络训练中常见的一类广泛的非凸问题。该问题的主要挑战在于不存在唯一极小值，这在非凸问题中无法保证。我们利用经典的基于Moreau包络的弱凸函数分析方法克服了这一挑战。完整的证明请参见附录(E)。现在，我们给出定理的陈述。</font></font></p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">定理（FedSGM：弱凸性）</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="246" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D453 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">f</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">凸</font></font><mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="247" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D454 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">克</font></font></mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">）：</font></font></strong></p>
</blockquote>

inherit;">本文提出了一种基于联邦高斯混合模型（FEDGMM）的联邦工具变量分析算法（FEDIV）。该算法将FEDGMM建模为一个由非凸非凹极小极大优化问题定义的联邦零和博弈。此外，本文还证明了该联邦博弈的解满足Stackelberg均衡，并在异质性偏差范围内满足客户端局部均衡。这项工作是首个利用联邦高斯混合模型进行联邦工具变量分析的研究。</font></font></p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">健全性</font></font><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">：</font></font></strong> <span class="note-content-value"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;"><font dir="auto" style="vertical-align: inherit;">3：良好</font></font></span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Presentation:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">3: good</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Contribution:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">3: good</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Strengths:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><ol>
<li>The work is the first work formulating federated IV via federated GMM. </li>
<li>The solid theoretical results strengthen the work. </li>
<li>The experimental results shows that the federated IV analysis framework is efficient in recovering the GMM estimators.</li>
</ol>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Weaknesses:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p>The experimental results in the main body of the paper are limited. The authors could move some of the results from the appendix for the final version of the draft.</p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Questions:</strong> <div class="note-content-value markdown-rendered"><p>In Table 1, when using GDA, Fed-DeepGMM performs closer to the DeepGMM compared to when SGDA is used for both. Is there an intuitive reason behind this observation?</p>
</div></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Flag For Ethics Review:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">No ethics review needed.</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Rating:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">6: marginally above the acceptance threshold. But would not mind if paper is rejected</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Confidence:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">3: You are fairly confident in your assessment. Math/other details were not carefully checked.</span></div><div><strong class="note-content-field disable-tex-rendering">Code Of Conduct:</strong> <span class="note-content-value">Yes</span></div></div></div><div class="ForumReplyForm_container__w2VwR"><div class="ForumReplyForm_buttons__eGXo_"><span class="ForumReplyForm_hint__azwm6">Add:</span><button type="button" class="ant-btn css-1enej14 css-var-_R_5b_ ant-btn-primary ant-btn-color-primary ant-btn-variant-solid ant-btn-sm" style="font-size: 0.75rem; <p><strong>Weakness (1,2) &amp; Question (2)</strong>: <em>Limited theory, GD, and non-convexity...</em></p>
</blockquote>
<p>Please refer to the <strong>Reformulation and Strengthened Theoretical Analysis</strong> in the general response.</p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Weakness (3)</strong>: <em>Fixed partial participation...</em></p>
</blockquote>
<p>We respectfully disagree with this point. We would like to clarify that assuming a fixed participation size <mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="261" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container> is a notational convention used in FL analysis, (see [1], [2], and [3]). Extending this to variable participation (<mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="262" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-mi class="mjx-i" size="s"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D461 TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mi>t</mi></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container>) is complementary to our core contribution of solving functional constraints. Our theoretical framework naturally supports this extension by simply bounding the sampling noise with the minimum participation count (<mjx-container class="MathJax CtxtMenu_Attached_0" jax="CHTML" tabindex="0" ctxtmenu_counter="263" style="font-size: 121.3%; position: relative;"><mjx-math class="MJX-TEX" aria-hidden="true"><mjx-msub><mjx-mi class="mjx-i"><mjx-c class="mjx-c1D45A TEX-I"></mjx-c></mjx-mi><mjx-script style="vertical-align: -0.15em;"><mjx-texatom size="s" texclass="ORD"><mjx-mo class="mjx-n"><mjx-c class="mjx-c6D"></mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c69"></mjx-c><mjx-c class="mjx-c6E"></mjx-c></mjx-mo></mjx-texatom></mjx-script></mjx-msub></mjx-math><mjx-assistive-mml unselectable="on" display="inline"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mrow data-mjx-texclass="ORD"><mo data-mjx-texclass="OP" movablelimits="true">min</mo></mrow></msub></math></mjx-assistive-mml></mjx-container>), without altering the convergence rate order.</p>
<p>References:</p>
<ol>
<li>Karimireddy et al., "SCAFFOLD: Stochastic Controlled Averaging for Federated Learning" (ICML 2020).</li>
<li>Li et al., "Federated Optimization in Heterogeneous Networks" (MLSys 2020).</li>
<li>Reddi et al., "Adaptive Federated Optimization" (ICLR 2021).</li>
</ol>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Weakness (4)</strong>: <em>Limited experiments...</em></p>
</blockquote>
<p>We acknowledge that our initial evaluation focused on Centralized methods. This choice was driven by the fact that no existing FL algorithm supports general functional constraints combined with partial participation and compression (please refer to our General Response). As we explained in the General Response, this is the first method to deal with functional constraints in FL and comparing against projection based, penalty based, and Frank-Wolfe methods may not be relevant. To show that, we conducted an additional experiment on NP Classification and compared against Penalty-FedAVG which is a heuristical method without any theoretical guarantee. The results for that is present in <a href="https://imgur.com/HPhwHK9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">New: Figure 1</a>. Here we show that penalty scheduling is a tedious task without any theoretical ground. If we try to tune the penalty coefficient to emulate the behaviour of hard switching, the problem becomes ill-conditioned as shown in the figure.</p>
<p>To further strengthen our claim, we run additional experiments on ADULT dataset with SGD and heterogeneous data distribution and compared the result against various centralized benchmarks to solve a fair classification problem.</p>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Question (1)</strong>: <em>Contribution of the work...</em></p>
</blockquote>
<p>We thank the reviewer for asking this question. The core deliverable of this work is to unlock the potential for realistic edge deployment with safety constraints. We showcase this in our experiments with Cartpole and NP classification as well. We provide a provable framework for Safe-FL on resource constrained edge. Prior to this work, one could have Safety (Constrained FL) or Efficiency (Compressed/Partial FL), but not both at the same time. Our work is the first to prove that safe, fair algorithms can be deployed on bandwidth-limited, unreliable edge networks. Furthermore, with the new theoretical results on SGD and weak convexity, we have significantly increased the practicality of our framework.</p>
